# Crate Plan: mizar-test

## Parser Task 46 Parse-Only Addendum

Parser Task 46 adds one active pass and one active fail source with exact
sidecars plus `spec.en.10.operator_declarations.parser` as a covered
`pass_and_fail` trace row. The pass case covers all three exact forms,
`left`/`right`/`none`, annotations, visibility, and definition-local placement;
the fail case covers malformed slots/delimiters and bounded recovery. The
runner and metadata changes are test-only: `mizar-test` production layout and
source hashes remain unchanged. Existing `.miz` sources and expectations are
not rebaselined, and no semantic operator behavior receives credit.

> Canonical language: English. Japanese companion: [../ja/00.crate_plan.md](../ja/00.crate_plan.md).

## Responsibility

Owned behavior:

- deterministic discovery of corpus payloads and `.expect.toml` sidecars;
- parsing and validation of expectation metadata and traceability metadata;
- staged-model admission and coverage accounting;
- active runner gates for stage-owned compiler seams;
- snapshot comparison/update policy and deterministic report surfaces;
- fail/soundness bookkeeping for tests that must not silently pass, disappear,
  or weaken.

Out of scope:

- compiler language semantics;
- name resolution, type checking, proof search, ATP, certificate replay, or
  kernel proof authority outside explicit consumer-runner seams;
- changing `doc/spec`, existing `.miz` tests, or existing expectation files to
  match current implementation behavior.

Observed behavior:

- metadata `plan` discovery, sidecar parsing, trace manifest parsing, duplicate
  id checks, missing source/sidecar checks, and deterministic plan ordering are
  implemented and tested;
- active `parse-only`, `declaration-symbol`, and `type-elaboration` runners
  have explicit active-tag gates and repository coverage;
- the active `type-elaboration` runner reaches checker-owned `BindingEnv`,
  reserve `DeclarationInput`, binding-specific builtin `set`/`object`
  `TypeExpressionInput`, and `DeclarationChecker` output through the
  checker-owned syntax-free task-48 seam, then continues to `TypedAst` and
  `ResolvedTypedAst` assembly for the supported source-derived reserve
  declaration slice, reads that real payload through `mizar-core`'s
  `ResolvedTypedAstSummary::from_ast`, prepares a binder-only `CoreContext`
  from the same real reserve bindings, extracts same-module attributed builtin
  reserve payloads far enough to report the checker `MissingEvidenceQuery`
  diagnostic, extracts same-module local mode reserve heads far enough to
  report the checker missing mode-expansion diagnostic, extracts same-module
  local structure reserve heads far enough to report the checker
  `MissingEvidenceQuery` diagnostic, extracts attributed same-module local
  structure reserve heads far enough to report the checker
  `MissingEvidenceQuery` diagnostic, extracts attributed same-module local mode
  reserve heads without supported real expansions or with mixed bare reserve
  uses far enough to report the checker missing mode-expansion diagnostic,
  extracts bare same-module no-argument local mode expansions from
  preceding bare-builtin RHS mode definitions far enough to add an active pass
  reserve fixture, extracts one-edge same-module local-mode chains when the
  dependency mode has an accepted bare-builtin RHS expansion, extracts real
  same-module local-mode expansions whose RHS is a same-module local structure
  head far enough to report the checker `MissingEvidenceQuery` diagnostic for
  missing base-shape/constructor-witness evidence, extracts real same-module
  local-mode expansions whose RHS is an attributed builtin head far enough to
  report the checker `MissingEvidenceQuery` diagnostic for missing
  attributed-type existential evidence, extracts attributed same-module
  local-mode reserve heads with real direct bare-builtin expansions far enough
  to report the same checker `MissingEvidenceQuery` diagnostic for missing
  attributed-type existential evidence, extracts attributed same-module
  local-mode reserve heads with real direct local-structure RHS expansions far
  enough to report the same checker `MissingEvidenceQuery` diagnostic for
  missing base-shape/constructor-witness and full attributed-type evidence,
  extracts attributed same-module local-mode reserve heads with real direct
  attributed-builtin RHS expansions far enough to report the same checker
  `MissingEvidenceQuery` diagnostic for missing full attributed-type evidence,
  extracts one-edge same-module local-mode chains ending in local-structure,
  attributed-builtin, bare-builtin attributed-root, structure-RHS
  attributed-root, and attributed-builtin-RHS attributed-root terminal payloads
  far enough to report the corresponding missing evidence-query diagnostics,
  reports forward same-module local-mode reserve heads that name later
  declarations as lower-stage active-range failures before checker handoff,
  and keeps mixed imports, imported attributes/structures, imported mode
  expansions beyond task 82's provenance bridge, and unsupported
  semantic/CoreIr/ControlFlowIr/VC/proof payload families on the external-gap
  key;
- architecture-22 matrix sidecar metadata is validated and reported as
  planned-only scenario coverage until owning consumers provide active
  clean/incremental/parallel/cache-race execution;
- transitional active parse-only `SurfaceAst` snapshot text baselines are
  validated and compared;
- general snapshot records, update mode, determinism mode, coverage reporting,
  pass/fail mix reporting, manifest ordering, and obsolete-reference checks
  are implemented;
- stage prerequisite validation is implemented;
- fail/soundness metadata bookkeeping validates certificate/kernel
  `rejection_reason`, recognized required soundness case shape/profile/phase,
  and mode-aware missing-case diagnostics; active proof/certificate/kernel
  execution remains paced by future consumer runners.

## Task Decomposition

### Task 1: Lint-policy guard

Purpose: keep `mizar-test` opted into workspace lints and require documented
lint exceptions.

Completion: committed as `0b24d1a`.

### Task 2: Source/spec gap audit and status sync

Purpose: record the audit baseline, classify drift/gaps, and synchronize module
status.

Completion: this crate plan plus `todo.md` status/follow-up updates.

Forbidden behavior: repairing `doc/spec`, existing `.miz`, or existing
expectations in this audit commit.

### Task 3: Runner modes and CLI completion

Purpose: complete validation-mode CLI behavior over corpus and coverage
metadata, including exit-code fixtures and deterministic output.

Spec refs: `minimal_crate.md`, `harness.md`.

Completion: validation-mode CLI parsing, strict/permissive unknown-root policy,
profile metadata retention/filtering, plan CLI fixtures, expectation-schema
fixtures, and harness documentation sync.

### Task 4: Snapshot API and canonicalization

Purpose: introduce the general snapshot module and canonical record API.

Spec refs: `snapshot.md`.

Completion: general in-memory `SnapshotRecord` API, canonical hash input,
line-ending normalization, local absolute path validation, deterministic
profile metadata ordering, and comparison diffs.

### Task 5: Snapshot update policy and determinism checks

Purpose: implement explicit update flow and repeat-render determinism checks.

Spec refs: `snapshot.md`.

Completion: explicit `VerifyOnly` versus `Update { reason }` baseline helper,
safe `.snap` baseline path validation, create/update round-trips, no-write
verify failures, and repeat-render determinism mismatch reports.

### Task 6: Coverage and pass/fail-mix reporting

Purpose: compute/report trace coverage, stored status drift, and corpus
pass/fail mix from discovery and traceability data.

Spec refs: `traceability.md`, architecture test strategy.

Completion: per-requirement and per-stage coverage reports, mode-aware
coverage/status gates, manifest id-order validation, obsolete-reference
diagnostics, link-validator regression fixtures, and deterministic CLI
pass/fail mix output.

### Task 7: Stage-prerequisite validation

Purpose: prevent higher-stage coverage credit before prerequisites are covered,
declared built-in, or accepted through `depends_on`.

Spec refs: `staged_model.md`, `traceability.md`.

Completion: manifest `depends_on` validation, `built_in` declarations,
stage-mismatch diagnostics, unsatisfied-prerequisite diagnostics, and
coverage-credit withholding before mode-aware completeness checks.

### Task 8: Fail/soundness contract support

Purpose: implement fail/soundness required-domain bookkeeping, typed failure
identity checks, false-arithmetic coverage, and weakening/deletion regression
rules.

Spec refs: `fail_soundness.md`.

Completion: certificate/kernel `rejection_reason` validation, recognized
`soundness.*` required-case shape/profile/phase validation, mode-aware
missing-case diagnostics for fail/soundness manifests, false-arithmetic stable
key gate, and regression fixtures. Real proof/certificate/kernel execution is
not fabricated before the owning consumer runners exist.

### Task 9: Corpus size and review-rule validation

Purpose: enforce corpus naming, size, class placement, generation metadata,
corpus-policy profile constraints, stress exclusion, and fuzz provenance rules.

Spec refs: `miz_corpus.md`.

Completion: `[origin]` provenance parsing/retention, generated/fuzz/property
placement gates, stress root discovery, profile constraints, fuzz-category
preservation, upper-bound `.miz` size diagnostics, naming diagnostics, and
metadata fixtures for clean and violating corpora.

### Task 10: Consumer-runner support

Purpose: keep discovery, expectations, stages, snapshots, active/planned gates,
and reporting synchronized with each consumer runner as it lands.

Spec refs: `harness.md`.

## Exit Criteria

Hard gates:

- no blocking `spec_gap`, accepted `repo_metadata_conflict`, or language
  behavior change remains hidden;
- no source behavior remains undocumented at crate exit;
- all task-owned gaps are fixed or explicitly deferred with a reason;
- `doc/design/mizar-test/en/` and `doc/design/mizar-test/ja/` stay
  synchronized;
- `doc/spec`, existing `.miz`, and existing expectations are untouched unless a
  future user request explicitly changes that intent.

Verification commands:

- `cargo test -p mizar-test`
- `cargo clippy -p mizar-test --all-targets -- -D warnings`
- `cargo fmt --check`

Review expectations:

- task-specific spec/docs review;
- test sufficiency review;
- implementation review;
- source/documentation consistency review.

definition

end;

### Exact Source Transactions

Both raw declaration environments are exactly symbols/labels/definitions/

R-032A freezes
`SurfaceResolvedArena::lower(&SurfaceAst, &ModuleId) ->
Result<SurfaceResolvedArena, SurfaceResolvedArenaError>` and total accessors
`source_id()`, `module()`, `arena()`, `resolved_node_for()`, and
`validate_against()`. It creates a one-to-one same-index structural arena
whose exact kind, children, range, recovery, root, source, and module match
the Surface tree. Each structural node has `SemanticOrigin` `[surface_id]`,
`NotApplicable`, and no key. Its fail-closed error rejects wrong source,
module, arena shape or node, stale maps, recovery/root mismatch, and checked
`u32` structural-path overflow without unwrap, saturation, or panic. R-032A
owns exactly `resolved_ast.rs`, `resolved_ast/tests.rs`, and the sole
`tests/lint_policy.rs` R-026 owning-spec entry for
`SurfaceResolvedArenaError` in a dedicated implementation commit. The exact
enum is linked from `doc/design/mizar-resolve/en/resolved_ast.md`; all
`node`/`child` payloads are `SurfaceNodeId`, including the frozen
`ResolutionStateMismatch { node: SurfaceNodeId }` and
`ReferenceKeyMismatch { node: SurfaceNodeId }` guards.

Implementation preflight classified the prior two-Rust-file ownership as
High `design_drift`, with no semantic `spec_gap`, because the existing
mandatory R-026 guard scans the new public enum. Its synchronized docs-only
scope correction is a separate commit before the exact three-Rust-file
implementation and changes no runner consumer, artifact, diagnostic,
semantics, or coverage state.

R-032B follows the exact declaration in
`doc/design/mizar-resolve/en/labels.md`:
`impl<'a> ProofLabelSourceCollector<'a>::new` receives
`ast: &'a SurfaceAst`, validation-only `module: &ModuleId`, owned
namespace/contribution, and `resolved: &'a SurfaceResolvedArena`, returning
`Result<Self, ProofLabelSourceCollectionError>`. `collect(&self)` returns
`Result<ProofLabelSourceCollection, ProofLabelSourceCollectionError>`.
Only ast/resolved are borrowed and stored under the same `'a`; module is not
stored. Collection accessors are `projections()` and `references()`. The
linked exact public non-exhaustive error wraps
`SurfaceResolvedArenaError`; both overflow variants carry
`node: SurfaceNodeId`. Checked `u32` scope/path conversion has no unwrap,
saturation, truncation, or panic. The validated arena is mandatory; neither
collector nor runner may fabricate a `ResolvedNodeId`, scope component, or
statement ordinal. R-032B implementation ownership is exactly
`crates/mizar-resolve/src/labels.rs`,
`crates/mizar-resolve/src/labels/tests.rs`, and the sole
`crates/mizar-resolve/tests/lint_policy.rs` R-026 owning-spec decision entry
for `ProofLabelSourceCollectionError` / `labels.md`.

The exact upper route is `Root -> CompilationUnit -> ItemList`: Root has
exactly one normal CompilationUnit structural child, CompilationUnit has
exactly one normal ItemList, and ItemList scans direct normal TheoremItem
children while skipping/no-descending every other item child. Each such
direct normal `TheoremItem` with a direct `ProofBlock` is a supported proof
root `[t]`, where `t` is its stable module source-order index; direct
supported nested proofs extend `[t, child, ...]` in source order. The exact
one-theorem B5C paths therefore remain `[0]`, `[0,0]`, `[0,1]`, and
`[0,0,0]`. Collection includes only an exact labelled `CompactStatement`,
the exact `JustificationClause -> ReferenceList -> Reference` chain for a
simple unqualified identifier under `by`, supported nested proofs, and
supported normal `CompactStatement`/`ConclusionStatement` rows. Broader
statement and label forms, qualified/grouped/bulk citations, indirect proof
owners, non-theorem items, and recovered/malformed nodes are excluded
without transferring any subtree to the checker.

One module-global one-based statement counter spans all supported theorem
roots without reset; theorem owners consume none. Normal supported
`CompactStatement`/`ConclusionStatement` rows consume ordinals in
depth-first preorder through supported nested proofs, and each reference
uses its owning statement's ordinal. Transparent containers and unsupported,
recovered, malformed, or excluded subtrees consume none and are not
descended. Visibility is the maximum ordinal in the entire labelled compact
subtree. Inner B5C consumes `1..5`: declaration 2, own-proof statement and
completion 3, following same-block statement 4, negative owning statement
5. Sibling consumes `1..6`, with the negative owning statement 6.

For each exact source the collection must contain one `ProofStep`,
`Private`, `LocalOnly` projection for `A`, contribution 0, declaration scope
`[0,0]`, visible only after completion ordinal 3, and token-node-8 source
provenance. It must contain one unqualified `ProofOrTheorem` candidate:
node 52/ordinal 5/scope `[0]` for inner-to-outer, or node 60/ordinal 6/scope
`[0,1]` for sibling. The positive boundary is a same-block citation after
completion; a self-reference from the label's own proof stays unresolved.
Same-spelling labels in separate top-level theorem roots neither conflict nor
resolve across roots. The isolation direction is an earlier theorem `[0]`
label cited from a later theorem `[1]`, which remains unresolved without
being masked by forward-reference behavior.

`LabelOriginPath` uses only the linked canonical collision-free
`proof-step-v1` length-framed one-line grammar. Package/module identity comes
from canonical `ModuleId`; owner/label identity is exact parser identifier
token text byte-for-byte with no case fold or Unicode transformation.
Owner/label same-spelling occurrences are zero-based and proof paths are
owner-relative. Projection `SemanticOrigin` paths
are exactly `[57,42,8]` and `[67,47,8]`; reference paths are exactly
`[57,55,52]` and `[67,63,60]`, with the exact token/reference anchors.
R-032B tests must prove provenance stability and uniqueness plus same-block
positive, own-proof self-reference, enclosing, sibling, and cross-theorem
boundaries.

The existing resolver must then produce index/table/ids `1/1/[0]`, zero
resolver diagnostics, and `has_unresolved = true`, with exactly one
`UnresolvedLabelRef` spelling `A`, at the frozen reference range, with
expectation `ProofOrTheorem`. No checker
`ResolvedAst`/`TypedAst` installation, keyed
`NodeResolutionState::Resolved` node, or `LabelResolution::Resolved` result,
statement/reference DTO row, or semantic/proof output is permitted. Every
Surface node remains syntax-owned.

### Default-deny collector and runner authentication amendment

R-032B follows the canonical closed Surface edge table. Its upper chain is
exactly `Root -> CompilationUnit -> ItemList -> direct TheoremItem`: Root
requires exactly one normal CompilationUnit structural child,
CompilationUnit exactly one normal ItemList, and ItemList scans direct normal
TheoremItem children while skipping/no-descending all other item children.
Each admitted theorem admits only its direct normal ProofBlock. A
`ProofBlock` admits only direct normal `CompactStatement` and
`ConclusionStatement` children. A compact statement inspects only its
proposition label, and either supported statement may descend only into
direct `ProofBlock` and `JustificationClause` children. The sole citation
shape is
`JustificationClause -> ReferenceList -> simple Reference -> sole identifier
token`. Formulae, tokens, wrappers, unsupported/recovered/malformed nodes,
qualified/grouped/bulk citations, and templates are default-denied with no
row, ordinal, or descent. Lower tests cover every allowed edge, including
each upper edge, positively. Upper negatives cover missing/additional/wrong
Root and CompilationUnit children, a theorem relocated directly beneath
Root or CompilationUnit, and a `VisibleItem`-wrapped theorem. Other
forbidden relocation is rejected. Mixed reference lists collect only exact
simple `Reference` siblings in source order; unsupported siblings add no row
or descent.

After selection solely by frozen source bytes plus the exact normal AST, the
active runner requires `env.module_id() == resolver.module`. It derives
namespace only with `NamespacePath::new(module.path().as_str())`, validates
every projection namespace, acquires exactly one contribution, and requires
id 0, kind `LocalSource`, the authenticated record module, and LocalSource
source id equal to public field `ast.source_id`. Every projection must match
the authenticated module, namespace, and contribution.

Focused runner tests independently mutate environment module; projection
module, namespace, and contribution id; contribution cardinality to zero and
multiple; contribution id; kind to each of `ImportedSource`, `Summary`, and
`Builtin`; record module; and LocalSource source id. Each mutation yields
only `declaration_symbol.label.proof_scope_input`, never confinement or any
public code. Expectation copying and mutation cannot select the branch.
The exact 48-file documentation scope and all baseline counts/hashes remain
unchanged.
